        Engineering brief produced from the codebase and task details:

        ### Task Summary
        The timeline agent's `image-to-video` (travel-between-images) task creation currently uses hardcoded defaults (model=wan-2.2, segment_frames=[49], frame_overlap=[2]) and ignores shot-level VideoTravelSettings stored in `shots.settings['travel-between-images']`. The task is to: (1) load shot-level travel settings from the DB when creating travel tasks, (2) surface those defaults in the system prompt so the LLM knows what's active, and (3) allow the agent to override them via tool arguments — following the same pattern used for the image generation alignment (imageContext).

        ### Key Evidence
        - The agent currently does NOT load any shot-level video travel settings from the database. The only settings loading is `loadProjectImageSettings` for image generation defaults. (source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/db.ts and loop.ts:321; relevance: high)
- VideoTravelSettings are stored per-shot in `shots.settings['travel-between-images']` JSONB column. The frontend loads them via `useShotSettings` → `fetchToolSettingsScopes` which cascades: defaults → user → project → shot. (source: src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useShotSettings.ts; relevance: high)
- The VideoTravelForm defaults include: selectedModel='wan-2.2', batchVideoFrames=61, batchVideoSteps=6, amountOfMotion=50, motionMode='basic', turboMode=false, enhancePrompt=false, loras=[], smoothContinuations=false, generationTypeMode='i2v', phaseConfig=undefined (source: src/tools/travel-between-images/settings.ts:158-194; relevance: high)
- buildTravelBetweenImagesInput() in generation.ts only passes: image_urls, base_prompts, segment_frames=[49], frame_overlap=[2], shot_id, model_name. It does NOT pass: steps, loras, amount_of_motion, phase_config, guidance_scale, turbo_mode, enhance_prompt, negative_prompt, text_before/after_prompts, or any other VideoTravelSettings fields. (source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/generation.ts:123-147; relevance: high)
- The downstream resolver (travelBetweenImages.ts) accepts ALL these fields: loras, amount_of_motion, phase_config, guidance_scale, steps, turbo_mode, enhance_prompt, negative_prompts, text_before/after_prompts, motion_mode, advanced_mode, structure_videos, etc. The resolver has full support — the gap is purely in the agent's input construction. (source: supabase/functions/create-task/resolvers/travelBetweenImages.ts:16-68; relevance: high)
- The image generation alignment pattern (already implemented) uses: (1) types in types.ts, (2) loading in db.ts, (3) context bag built in loop.ts:331-336, (4) surfaced in prompts.ts:43-67, (5) applied in create-task.ts:133-167. Travel needs an analogous 'travelContext' flow. (source: loop.ts:320-344, create-task.ts:58-61, prompts.ts:34-67; relevance: high)
- The agent already resolves shot_id from selected clips via findShotForGenerations() in create-task.ts:114. This same shotId can be used to query shots.settings for travel defaults. (source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts:113-114; relevance: medium)
- MODEL_SPEC_REGISTRY defines per-model defaults: wan-2.2 (61 frames, 6 steps, no guidance), ltx-2.3 (97 frames, 30 steps, guidance 3.0), ltx-2.3-fast (97 frames, 8 steps, guidance 3.0). These should inform the agent's defaults when no shot settings exist. (source: src/tools/travel-between-images/modelCapabilities.ts:80-162; relevance: medium)
- The create_task tool schema currently has no travel-specific override fields beyond 'model'. Fields like amount_of_motion, loras, steps, guidance_scale, enhance_prompt etc. are not in the schema. (source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tool-schemas.ts:55-60; relevance: high)
- The resolver's DEFAULT_TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES_VALUES has model_name='wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2', steps=20, amount_of_motion=0.5, seed=789. These differ from the form defaults (steps=6 for wan-2.2, amountOfMotion=50 which maps to 0.5). (source: supabase/functions/create-task/resolvers/travelBetweenImages.ts:70-82; relevance: medium)

        ### Test Expectations
        - No explicit test expectations captured.

        ### Constraints
        - Must not break existing image generation context (imageContext) flow — travel context is additive
- The agent edge function runs in Deno (supabase/functions/) — cannot import frontend TS modules from src/ directly. Travel settings normalization logic must be duplicated or extracted to a shared edge-function-compatible module
- Shot-level settings are stored as JSONB in shots.settings['travel-between-images'] — must handle null/missing gracefully
- Amount of motion is 0-100 in UI settings but 0-1 in the resolver — must normalize when passing through
- LoRAs are stored as ShotLora[] (with id, name, path, strength) in settings but the resolver expects Array<{path, strength}> — must map
- Model name mapping: UI uses 'wan-2.2'/'ltx-2.3'/'ltx-2.3-fast', worker uses 'wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2'/'ltx2_22B'/'ltx2_22B_distilled' — normalizeTravelModelName() already handles this
- System prompt must stay concise — travel defaults section should be <100 tokens, only surface what the LLM needs to know (model, LoRA names, motion level)
- Tool schema additionalProperties is false — any new override fields must be explicitly added to the schema
- The create-task edge function must be redeployed after changes

        ### Suggested Approach
        Follow the imageContext pattern with a parallel travelContext:

1. **Types** (types.ts): Add `AgentTravelContext` interface with fields: `defaultModel: string`, `steps: number`, `frames: number`, `amountOfMotion: number`, `loras: Array<{name: string, path: string, strength: number}>`, `guidanceScale?: number`, `enhancePrompt: boolean`, `phaseConfig?: Record<string,unknown>`, `turboMode: boolean`, `negativePrompt?: string`, `textBeforePrompts?: string`, `textAfterPrompts?: string`.

2. **DB loading** (db.ts): Add `loadShotTravelSettings(supabaseAdmin, shotId)` that queries `shots.settings` for `['travel-between-images']` key, normalizes the JSONB (mirror the essential normalization from settings.ts), and returns `AgentTravelContext | null`. Also duplicate the model defaults constants (frames/steps per model) since we can't import from src/.

3. **Loop wiring** (loop.ts): After the existing imageContext block (~line 336), load travel settings using the already-resolved `clipShotId`. Build `travelContext` bag. Pass to both `buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt()` and `processToolCalls()`.

4. **System prompt** (prompts.ts): Add a travel defaults section when travelContext is present. Surface: preferred model, active LoRAs (by name), motion level, steps. Keep it brief — the LLM just needs to know what's configured so it can mention it and respect it.

5. **Tool schema** (tool-schemas.ts): Add optional fields to create_task for travel overrides: `amount_of_motion` (number 0-100), `steps` (number), `guidance_scale` (number), `enhance_prompt` (boolean). Don't expose all 30+ fields — just the ones a user would reasonably ask to change.

6. **create-task.ts**: Accept `travelContext?: AgentTravelContext`. When task_type is `image-to-video`, merge travelContext defaults into the params passed to `createGenerationTask()`. Agent-provided overrides take precedence over travelContext, which takes precedence over hardcoded defaults.

7. **generation.ts**: Expand `buildTravelBetweenImagesInput()` to accept and pass through all the new fields from params: loras, amount_of_motion (divide by 100), steps, guidance_scale, enhance_prompt, negative_prompts, phase_config, turbo_mode, text_before/after_prompts. The resolver already accepts all of these.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Make the timeline agent's travel-between-images (video travel) task creation match the VideoTravelForm behavior — load and use all default settings (model, LoRAs, motion params, etc.), surface them in the system prompt so the LLM knows what defaults are active, and allow the agent to override them based on user requests. Similar pattern to the image generation alignment done in make-the-timeline-agent-s-20260406-0602.

User notes and answers:
- ## Research Findings: Video Travel Settings & Agent Gap Analysis

### Where video travel settings are stored
- **Table:** shots.settings JSONB column, keyed by tool_id 'travel-between-images'
- **Scope:** Per-shot (shot > project > user > defaults cascade)
- **NOT in projects.settings** like image gen — this is a different storage path
- Settings loaded via useAutoSaveSettings → useToolSettings → shots.settings['travel-between-images']

### Key defaults the agent needs to know about
From settings.ts defaults (lines 158-194):
- selectedModel: 'wan-2.2' (options: wan-2.2, ltx-2.3, ltx-2.3-fast)
- batchVideoFrames: 61 (4N+1 format)
- batchVideoSteps: 6
- guidanceScale: undefined
- generationTypeMode: 'i2v' (i2v vs vace)
- amountOfMotion: 50 (0-100, converted to 0-1)
- motionMode: 'basic' vs 'advanced'
- turboMode: false
- smoothContinuations: false
- enhancePrompt: false
- loras: [] (Array<ShotLora> with id, name, path, strength, previewImageUrl, trigger_word)
- phaseConfig: undefined (advanced mode only)
- ltxHdResolution: true
- modelSettingsByModel: per-model frame/step overrides

### What the form sends to createGenerationTask (requestBody.ts lines 39-152)
- model_name (normalized)
- base_prompts, negative_prompts, text_before/after_prompts
- segment_frames, frame_overlap
- phase_config (if advanced mode)
- amount_of_motion
- loras: Array<{ path: string; strength: number }>
- pair_loras (per-pair overrides)
- turbo_mode, guidance_scale
- continuation_config (if smooth continuations)
- structure_guidance, travel_guidance
- generation_mode ('timeline', 'batch', 'by-pair')

### Current agent gaps for video travel
1. NO LoRA support at all — no schema field, no parsing, no loading, no passing
2. Model defaults not loaded from shot settings — always falls back to wan-2.2
3. No motion params (amountOfMotion, turboMode) exposed to agent
4. No frame/step count awareness
5. No guidance_scale support
6. No generationTypeMode (i2v vs vace) awareness
7. No smooth continuation support
8. image-to-video model default NOT pulled from imageContext (create-task.ts lines 133-135 only defaults for text-to-image)

### LoRA details for video travel
- ShotLora type: { id, name, path, strength, previewImageUrl?, trigger_word? }
- Stored in shots.settings['travel-between-images'].loras
- Multi-stage LoRAs have lowNoisePath for phase routing (high-noise early, low-noise final)
- Downstream create-task edge function DOES support loras param (travelBetweenImages.ts)
- Supports pair_loras for per-segment overrides

### How to load shot settings from the agent
The agent knows the shot context from selected clips (via resolveClipGenerationIds → findShotForGenerations).
To load shot settings: supabaseAdmin.from('shots').select('settings').eq('id', shotId).maybeSingle()
Then extract: data?.settings?.['travel-between-images']

### What the downstream create-task edge function accepts for travel
From supabase/functions/create-task/travelBetweenImages.ts:
- loras?: Array<{ path: string; strength: number }>
- pair_loras?: (Array<...> | null)[]
- model_name (normalized via normalizeTravelModelName)
- amount_of_motion
- turbo_mode
- guidance_scale
- phase_config
- continuation_config

### Standardization opportunity (USER REQUEST)
The image gen alignment and this video travel alignment share the same pattern:
1. Load tool-specific settings from DB
2. Surface defaults in system prompt
3. Thread settings through to task execution
4. Allow LLM to override based on user requests

Currently image gen uses:
- AgentProjectImageSettings type
- loadProjectImageSettings() from projects.settings
- imageContext bag with defaultModelName + activeReference
- Threaded through loop → processToolCalls → executeToolCall → executeCreateTask

For standardization, consider:
- Rename imageContext → agentToolContext or generationContext
- Add videoTravelSettings alongside image settings
- loadShotVideoTravelSettings() parallel to loadProjectImageSettings()
- Shared pattern: { defaults loaded from DB, surfaced in prompt, overridable by LLM }
- Single context bag that carries both image and video travel defaults
- buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt accepts both contexts
- executeCreateTask uses appropriate context based on task_type family

### Files to modify (agent-side)
All in supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/:
- types.ts — Add AgentVideoTravelSettings, rename/expand context types
- db.ts — Add loadShotVideoTravelSettings()
- tool-schemas.ts — Add loras, motion params, frame/step fields for i2v tasks
- prompts.ts — Surface video travel defaults in system prompt
- tools/create-task.ts — Pass video travel params through for i2v tasks
- loop.ts — Load video travel settings, build expanded context
- generation.ts — Accept and pass LoRA + motion params in buildTravelBetweenImagesInput

### Scope boundaries
- NOT doing: per-pair LoRA overrides (too complex for agent v1), structure guidance/ControlNet, steerable motion advanced settings, pair-level prompt overrides
- Phase config: Only expose through generationTypeMode (i2v/vace) toggle, not raw phase config editing
- LoRAs: expose what's configured in shot settings, allow LLM to note them, don't add/remove LoRAs
- USER REQUEST: Standardize the pattern between image gen settings and video travel settings. Don't just duplicate the imageContext pattern — refactor it so both share a common structure. Rename imageContext to something broader (e.g., generationContext) that carries both image and video travel defaults. The goal is one clean pattern that works for any tool's settings the agent needs to be aware of.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Agent Video Travel Settings Alignment & Context Unification

## Overview

The timeline agent's `image-to-video` task creation hardcodes minimal parameters (segment_frames=[49], frame_overlap=[2]) and ignores shot-level VideoTravelSettings stored in `shots.settings['travel-between-images']`. The downstream resolver (`travelBetweenImages.ts`) already accepts all fields — the gap is purely in the agent's input construction.

**Goal:** Load shot-level travel settings from DB, surface them in the system prompt, thread them through task creation, and allow LLM overrides — while unifying the context pattern with image generation into a single `GenerationContext`.

**Key constraint:** The agent runs in Deno (edge functions) — cannot import from `src/`. Model defaults and normalization must be duplicated in the edge function.

**Files touched (all under `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/`):**
- `types.ts` — new types
- `db.ts` — new DB loader
- `loop.ts` — wiring
- `prompts.ts` — system prompt section
- `tool-schemas.ts` — new optional fields
- `tools/create-task.ts` — context threading + merge
- `tools/generation.ts` — pass-through fields

## Phase 1: Types & DB Loading

### Step 1: Add travel context types (`types.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `AgentVideoTravelSettings` interface:
   ```ts
   export interface AgentVideoTravelSettings {
     selectedModel: string;       // 'wan-2.2' | 'ltx-2.3' | 'ltx-2.3-fast'
     frames: number;              // batchVideoFrames (4N+1)
     steps: number;               // batchVideoSteps
     amountOfMotion: number;      // 0-100 UI scale
     guidanceScale?: number;
     turboMode: boolean;
     enhancePrompt: boolean;
     negativePrompt?: string;
     textBeforePrompts?: string;
     textAfterPrompts?: string;
     generationTypeMode: 'i2v' | 'vace';
     loras: Array<{ name: string; path: string; strength: number }>;
     phaseConfig?: Record<string, unknown>;
     smoothContinuations: boolean;
   }
   ```
2. **Add** unified `GenerationContext` type that replaces `CreateTaskImageContext`:
   ```ts
   export interface GenerationContext {
     image: {
       defaultModelName?: AgentTextToImageModel;
       activeReference?: ResolvedReference | null;
     } | null;
     travel: AgentVideoTravelSettings | null;
   }
   ```

### Step 2: Add travel settings DB loader (`db.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `loadShotVideoTravelSettings(supabaseAdmin, shotId)` function that:
   - Queries `shots.settings` for the given `shotId`
   - Extracts `settings['travel-between-images']` from the JSONB
   - Normalizes it into `AgentVideoTravelSettings` (mirror the essential normalization logic from `src/tools/travel-between-images/settings.ts:368-410`, but simplified for the fields we care about)
   - Returns `AgentVideoTravelSettings | null`
2. **Duplicate** model defaults as a simple constant (don't import from src):
   ```ts
   const TRAVEL_MODEL_DEFAULTS: Record<string, { frames: number; steps: number; guidanceScale?: number }> = {
     'wan-2.2': { frames: 61, steps: 6 },
     'ltx-2.3': { frames: 97, steps: 30, guidanceScale: 3 },
     'ltx-2.3-fast': { frames: 97, steps: 8, guidanceScale: 3 },
   };
   ```
3. **Handle** model-specific overrides via `modelSettingsByModel` from shot settings (if the user customized frames/steps for a specific model, use those over the hardcoded defaults).
4. **Handle** null/missing gracefully — if no shot settings exist, return `null` (fall back to hardcoded defaults later in the chain).

### Step 3: Add tool schema fields for travel overrides (`tool-schemas.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** optional fields to `create_task` properties (after existing fields, before `additionalProperties: false`):
   - `amount_of_motion`: `{ type: "number", description: "Motion intensity 0-100 for image-to-video. Higher = more motion." }`
   - `steps`: `{ type: "number", description: "Inference steps for image-to-video. Model-dependent (wan-2.2: 6, ltx-2.3: 30, ltx-2.3-fast: 8)." }`
   - `guidance_scale`: `{ type: "number", description: "Guidance scale for image-to-video (LTX models only)." }`
   - `enhance_prompt`: `{ type: "boolean", description: "Auto-enhance prompts for image-to-video." }`
   - `turbo_mode`: `{ type: "boolean", description: "Turbo mode for wan-2.2 image-to-video (faster, lower quality)." }`

Keep the schema lean — these are the fields a user would reasonably ask the agent to change. Don't expose phase_config, LoRAs (LoRAs come from shot settings, not LLM choices), smooth_continuations, etc.

## Phase 2: Wiring & Integration

### Step 4: Refactor context types and wire loop (`loop.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** `CreateTaskImageContext` import with `GenerationContext` from `types.ts`.
2. **After** the existing imageContext block (~line 331-336), add travel settings loading:
   - Use the already-resolved `clipShotId` to call `loadShotVideoTravelSettings(supabaseAdmin, clipShotId)`.
   - If no `clipShotId` exists (no selected clips), travel settings will be `null` — that's fine, the hardcoded defaults in `generation.ts` still apply.
3. **Build** unified `GenerationContext`:
   ```ts
   const generationContext: GenerationContext = {
     image: imageSettings ? { defaultModelName: imageSettings.selectedTextModel ?? "qwen-image", activeReference } : null,
     travel: travelSettings,
   };
   ```
4. **Update** `buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt()` call to pass `travelSettings`.
5. **Update** `processToolCalls()` and `executeToolCall()` to accept `GenerationContext` instead of `CreateTaskImageContext`.
6. **Update** `executeCreateTask()` call to pass `generationContext`.

### Step 5: Surface travel defaults in system prompt (`prompts.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `travelSettings?: AgentVideoTravelSettings | null` to the options parameter of `buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt()`.
2. **Add** a compact travel defaults block when travelSettings is present (keep under ~100 tokens):
   ```
   Active video travel defaults: model={selectedModel}, frames={frames}, steps={steps}, motion={amountOfMotion}%{loraLine}{guidanceLine}
   Use these for image-to-video unless the user specifies otherwise.
   ```
   - Only include LoRA line if loras.length > 0: `, loras=[{name1}, {name2}]`
   - Only include guidance line if guidanceScale is set: `, guidance={guidanceScale}`
3. **Insert** after the existing model guide section, before task guide.

### Step 6: Thread travel context through create-task (`tools/create-task.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** `CreateTaskImageContext` with `GenerationContext` in the function signature.
2. **Extract** image and travel sub-contexts: `const imageContext = generationContext?.image; const travelContext = generationContext?.travel;`
3. **For** `image-to-video` task type, build travel params from the merge chain (agent args > travelContext > hardcoded defaults):
   ```ts
   const travelParams = taskType === 'image-to-video' ? {
     amount_of_motion: asFiniteNumber(args.amount_of_motion) ?? (travelContext ? travelContext.amountOfMotion / 100 : undefined),
     steps: asPositiveNumber(args.steps) ?? travelContext?.steps,
     guidance_scale: asFiniteNumber(args.guidance_scale) ?? travelContext?.guidanceScale,
     enhance_prompt: typeof args.enhance_prompt === 'boolean' ? args.enhance_prompt : travelContext?.enhancePrompt,
     turbo_mode: typeof args.turbo_mode === 'boolean' ? args.turbo_mode : travelContext?.turboMode,
     loras: travelContext?.loras?.map(l => ({ path: l.path, strength: l.strength })),
     negative_prompts: travelContext?.negativePrompt ? [travelContext.negativePrompt] : undefined,
     text_before_prompts: travelContext?.textBeforePrompts || undefined,
     text_after_prompts: travelContext?.textAfterPrompts || undefined,
     segment_frames: travelContext ? [travelContext.frames] : undefined,
     generation_type_mode: travelContext?.generationTypeMode,
     smooth_continuations: travelContext?.smoothContinuations,
     phase_config: travelContext?.phaseConfig,
   } : undefined;
   ```
4. **Pass** travelParams into `createGenerationTask()` via the `params` field (the existing `legacyInput` spread in `buildTravelBetweenImagesInput` will pick them up).
5. **Default model** for image-to-video: use `travelContext?.selectedModel` when agent doesn't specify a model:
   ```ts
   const defaultModelName = taskType === 'image-to-video'
     ? travelContext?.selectedModel
     : (taskType === "text-to-image" || Boolean(TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType]))
       ? imageContext?.defaultModelName
       : undefined;
   ```

### Step 7: Expand travel input builder (`tools/generation.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** `buildTravelBetweenImagesInput()` to extract and pass through additional fields from `legacyInput` (which now carries travelParams via `args.params`):
   - `loras` → pass as-is (Array<{path, strength}>)
   - `amount_of_motion` → pass as-is (already 0-1 from create-task normalization)
   - `steps` → pass as-is
   - `guidance_scale` → pass as-is
   - `enhance_prompt` → pass as boolean
   - `turbo_mode` → pass as boolean
   - `negative_prompts` → pass as string array
   - `text_before_prompts` → pass as string
   - `text_after_prompts` → pass as string
   - `model_type` → map from generation_type_mode ('i2v'/'vace')
   - `phase_config` → pass as record (only when advanced/vace mode)
2. **Update** the return object to include these fields alongside existing ones.
3. **Keep** `segment_frames` and `frame_overlap` fallback defaults ([49] and [2]) when not provided by travelParams.

## Phase 3: Cleanup & Validation

### Step 8: Remove old `CreateTaskImageContext` export and update imports
**Scope:** Small
1. **Remove** `CreateTaskImageContext` interface from `tools/create-task.ts` (replaced by `GenerationContext` in `types.ts`).
2. **Update** import in `loop.ts` to use `GenerationContext` from `types.ts` instead.
3. **Verify** no other files import `CreateTaskImageContext`.

### Step 9: Manual smoke test and deploy
**Scope:** Small
1. **Verify** TypeScript compiles cleanly: `cd reigh-app && npx tsc --noEmit --project supabase/functions/tsconfig.json` (or equivalent check).
2. **Test scenarios** (manual):
   - Agent creates image-to-video task with no shot settings → falls back to hardcoded defaults (wan-2.2, 49 frames, 2 overlap) — behavior unchanged.
   - Agent creates image-to-video task with shot settings containing custom model (ltx-2.3), LoRAs, motion=75 → all threaded through.
   - Agent overrides model via tool arg → agent arg wins over shot settings.
   - System prompt shows travel defaults section when settings exist.
   - Image generation tasks still work identically (imageContext path unchanged).
3. **Deploy** the agent edge function: `npx supabase functions deploy ai-timeline-agent --project-ref wczysqzxlwdndgxitrvc`

## Execution Order
1. Types first (Step 1) — everything depends on these.
2. DB loader (Step 2) — independent of other changes, needed by loop.
3. Tool schema (Step 3) — independent, needed for agent overrides.
4. Loop + prompt wiring (Steps 4-5) — depends on types + DB loader.
5. Create-task + generation threading (Steps 6-7) — depends on types + context shape.
6. Cleanup + test (Steps 8-9) — after everything compiles.

## Validation Order
1. TypeScript compilation check after each step.
2. Full compile after Step 8.
3. Manual smoke tests in Step 9.

## Notes on Image Gen Gaps
The user asked to "think bigger" about image gen gaps. The main known gap is **image LoRAs** — covered by the related plan `fix-lora-passthrough-form-20260406-0645`. This plan's `GenerationContext` type should leave room for image LoRAs to be added later (the `image` sub-object can be extended). Other potential image gen gaps worth noting but NOT in scope:
- **Negative prompts** for image generation (currently not threaded)
- **Image dimensions/aspect ratio** overrides
- **Batch count** defaults from project settings

These should be filed as separate follow-ups rather than bloating this plan.

        A researcher recommended you consider these points in executing the task:

        - [CRITICAL] clipShotId resolution gated behind imageSettings — travel settings won't load when no project image settings exist
  In loop.ts lines 322-327, clipGenerationIds and clipShotId are only resolved when `imageSettings` is truthy. resolveClipGenerationIds() doesn't actually use imageSettings — it only needs selectedClips, registry, and config. But because of this guard, if a project has video travel settings on a shot but NO project-level image settings, clipShotId will be null and loadShotVideoTravelSettings() will never receive a valid shot ID. The plan's Step 4 says to 'use the already-resolved clipShotId' — but this means travel settings fail silently for users who haven't configured image gen. Fix: resolve clipGenerationIds and clipShotId unconditionally (when selected clips exist), independent of imageSettings.
  Source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/loop.ts:322-327
- [CRITICAL] amount_of_motion from LLM tool args not converted from 0-100 to 0-1 scale
  The plan's Step 6 merges: `amount_of_motion: asFiniteNumber(args.amount_of_motion) ?? (travelContext ? travelContext.amountOfMotion / 100 : undefined)`. The tool schema (Step 3) tells the LLM the range is 0-100. But only the context fallback path divides by 100 — the LLM arg path passes through raw. The resolver and form both expect 0-1 scale (confirmed by test at generateVideoService.test.ts:1480 and resolver default 0.5). If the LLM passes 80 (from user saying 'motion 80'), it hits the resolver as 80.0 instead of 0.8. Fix: always divide by 100 regardless of source, or change the schema to 0-1.
  Source: supabase/functions/create-task/resolvers/travelBetweenImages.ts:81, plan Step 6
- [IMPORTANT] smooth_continuations boolean won't work — resolver expects continuation_config object
  The plan's Step 6 lists `smooth_continuations: travelContext?.smoothContinuations` in travelParams. But the resolver's TravelBetweenImagesTaskInput has no `smooth_continuations` field — it expects `continuation_config: { strategy: string, overlap_frames: number }`. The form builds this via resolveGenerationPolicy() which determines strategy and overlap frames from the model spec and settings. Passing a boolean will be silently ignored. Fix: either build the continuation_config object from the smoothContinuations boolean + model spec, or drop smooth_continuations from the plan (the plan already says it's not a tool arg).
  Source: src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/generateVideo/requestBody.ts:101-105, supabase/functions/create-task/resolvers/travelBetweenImages.ts:21
- [IMPORTANT] generation_type_mode field name doesn't match resolver's expected field name (model_type)
  The plan stores `generationTypeMode: 'i2v' | 'vace'` in AgentVideoTravelSettings and Step 6 passes `generation_type_mode: travelContext?.generationTypeMode` to travelParams. But the resolver field is `model_type`, not `generation_type_mode`. The spread via `...legacyInput` in buildTravelBetweenImagesInput would pass it as `generation_type_mode` which the resolver ignores (it looks for `model_type`). Fix: rename to `model_type` when building travelParams.
  Source: supabase/functions/create-task/resolvers/travelBetweenImages.ts:34
- [IMPORTANT] buildTravelBetweenImagesInput already spreads ...legacyInput — Step 7's explicit field extraction is redundant
  generation.ts line 138 does `return { ...legacyInput, image_urls, base_prompts, segment_frames, frame_overlap, ... }`. Since the plan puts travel params into `args.params` (which becomes legacyInput), ALL fields are already spread into the output. Step 7's proposal to 'extract and pass through' each field individually is unnecessary work. Worse, explicitly setting fields that are also in the spread could mask bugs (the explicit value silently overrides the spread). The only changes needed in generation.ts are handling fields that need transformation (like model_name normalization, which already exists).
  Source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/generation.ts:138-146
- [IMPORTANT] Plan's TRAVEL_MODEL_DEFAULTS steps don't match resolver defaults — risk of changing behavior
  The plan proposes `'wan-2.2': { frames: 61, steps: 6 }` as model defaults. But the resolver's DEFAULT_TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES_VALUES has `steps: 20` (line 73). Currently the agent sends no steps at all, so the resolver uses 20. If the plan's DB loader returns `steps: 6` as a 'default' when no shot settings exist, it would change the behavior from 20 steps to 6 steps. The plan says 'return null when no settings exist' which avoids this, but the TRAVEL_MODEL_DEFAULTS constant is used for normalization WITHIN loadShotVideoTravelSettings — if shot settings exist but have no steps value, using 6 instead of 20 changes behavior.
  Source: supabase/functions/create-task/resolvers/travelBetweenImages.ts:70-73
- [MINOR] Plan proposes modifying executeCreateTask signature but generation.ts is the actual task builder
  The plan's Step 6 shows travel params being built in create-task.ts and 'passed into createGenerationTask() via the params field.' But createGenerationTask() just forwards to buildTravelBetweenImagesInput() which builds the final input. The plan should be clear that travelParams go into the `params` field of CreateGenerationTaskArgs, not as top-level args, since the existing code merges params via legacyInput spread.
  Source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/generation.ts:123-147
- [MINOR] Plan Step 3 adds additionalProperties: false constraint note but schema may not have it
  Step 3 says 'after existing fields, before additionalProperties: false'. The actual schema in tool-schemas.ts should be verified to have this constraint — if it doesn't, adding fields without updating the constraint is fine. If it does, the new fields must be added before it to avoid schema validation rejecting them.
  Source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tool-schemas.ts

        Debt guidance:
        - These are known accepted limitations. Do not re-flag them unless the current plan makes them worse, broadens them, or fails to contain them.
        - Prefix every new concern with a subsystem tag followed by a colon, for example `Timeout recovery: retry backoff remains brittle`.
        - When a concern is recurring debt that still needs to be flagged, prefix it with `Recurring debt:` after the subsystem tag, for example `Timeout recovery: Recurring debt: retry backoff remains brittle`.

Read this file first — it contains 8 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: heavy. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.
        - Over-engineering: prefer the simplest approach that fully solves the problem.
        - Reuse existing flag IDs when the same concern is still open.
        - `verified_flag_ids`: list flag IDs from prior iterations that the revised plan actually resolves (e.g., if the plan was revised to fix FLAG-001, and you confirm the fix is correct, include "FLAG-001"). Only include flags you've verified — don't guess.

> DEVELOPER

You are an independent reviewer. Critique the plan against the actual repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace

        Engineering brief produced from the codebase and task details:

        ### Task Summary
        The timeline agent's `image-to-video` (travel-between-images) task creation currently uses hardcoded defaults (model=wan-2.2, segment_frames=[49], frame_overlap=[2]) and ignores shot-level VideoTravelSettings stored in `shots.settings['travel-between-images']`. The task is to: (1) load shot-level travel settings from the DB when creating travel tasks, (2) surface those defaults in the system prompt so the LLM knows what's active, and (3) allow the agent to override them via tool arguments — following the same pattern used for the image generation alignment (imageContext).

        ### Key Evidence
        - The agent currently does NOT load any shot-level video travel settings from the database. The only settings loading is `loadProjectImageSettings` for image generation defaults. (source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/db.ts and loop.ts:321; relevance: high)
- VideoTravelSettings are stored per-shot in `shots.settings['travel-between-images']` JSONB column. The frontend loads them via `useShotSettings` → `fetchToolSettingsScopes` which cascades: defaults → user → project → shot. (source: src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useShotSettings.ts; relevance: high)
- The VideoTravelForm defaults include: selectedModel='wan-2.2', batchVideoFrames=61, batchVideoSteps=6, amountOfMotion=50, motionMode='basic', turboMode=false, enhancePrompt=false, loras=[], smoothContinuations=false, generationTypeMode='i2v', phaseConfig=undefined (source: src/tools/travel-between-images/settings.ts:158-194; relevance: high)
- buildTravelBetweenImagesInput() in generation.ts only passes: image_urls, base_prompts, segment_frames=[49], frame_overlap=[2], shot_id, model_name. It does NOT pass: steps, loras, amount_of_motion, phase_config, guidance_scale, turbo_mode, enhance_prompt, negative_prompt, text_before/after_prompts, or any other VideoTravelSettings fields. (source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/generation.ts:123-147; relevance: high)
- The downstream resolver (travelBetweenImages.ts) accepts ALL these fields: loras, amount_of_motion, phase_config, guidance_scale, steps, turbo_mode, enhance_prompt, negative_prompts, text_before/after_prompts, motion_mode, advanced_mode, structure_videos, etc. The resolver has full support — the gap is purely in the agent's input construction. (source: supabase/functions/create-task/resolvers/travelBetweenImages.ts:16-68; relevance: high)
- The image generation alignment pattern (already implemented) uses: (1) types in types.ts, (2) loading in db.ts, (3) context bag built in loop.ts:331-336, (4) surfaced in prompts.ts:43-67, (5) applied in create-task.ts:133-167. Travel needs an analogous 'travelContext' flow. (source: loop.ts:320-344, create-task.ts:58-61, prompts.ts:34-67; relevance: high)
- The agent already resolves shot_id from selected clips via findShotForGenerations() in create-task.ts:114. This same shotId can be used to query shots.settings for travel defaults. (source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts:113-114; relevance: medium)
- MODEL_SPEC_REGISTRY defines per-model defaults: wan-2.2 (61 frames, 6 steps, no guidance), ltx-2.3 (97 frames, 30 steps, guidance 3.0), ltx-2.3-fast (97 frames, 8 steps, guidance 3.0). These should inform the agent's defaults when no shot settings exist. (source: src/tools/travel-between-images/modelCapabilities.ts:80-162; relevance: medium)
- The create_task tool schema currently has no travel-specific override fields beyond 'model'. Fields like amount_of_motion, loras, steps, guidance_scale, enhance_prompt etc. are not in the schema. (source: supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tool-schemas.ts:55-60; relevance: high)
- The resolver's DEFAULT_TRAVEL_BETWEEN_IMAGES_VALUES has model_name='wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2', steps=20, amount_of_motion=0.5, seed=789. These differ from the form defaults (steps=6 for wan-2.2, amountOfMotion=50 which maps to 0.5). (source: supabase/functions/create-task/resolvers/travelBetweenImages.ts:70-82; relevance: medium)

        ### Relevant Code
        | File | Functions | Why |
| --- | --- | --- |
| reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/generation.ts | buildTravelBetweenImagesInput, normalizeTravelModelName, CreateGenerationTaskArgs | Primary gap: constructs travel payload with only minimal fields. Needs to accept and pass through all VideoTravelSettings fields (loras, steps, amount_of_motion, phase_config, guidance_scale, etc.) |
| reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tools/create-task.ts | executeCreateTask, CreateTaskImageContext | Orchestrates task creation. Needs a new 'travelContext' (analogous to imageContext) that carries shot-level travel defaults and applies them as fallbacks when building travel tasks. |
| reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/loop.ts | runAgentLoop (lines 320-344) | Wiring point: loads settings and builds context bags. Needs to also load shot-level travel settings and build a travelContext, then pass to prompts and tool execution. |
| reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/db.ts | loadProjectImageSettings, loadActiveReference | Database loading. Needs a new function like `loadShotTravelSettings(supabaseAdmin, shotId)` to query shots.settings['travel-between-images'] and normalize it. |
| reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/prompts.ts | buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt | System prompt construction. Needs to surface active travel defaults (model, LoRAs, motion, steps) so the LLM knows what settings are in effect and can mention them to the user. |

| reigh-app/supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/tool-schemas.ts | TIMELINE_AGENT_TOOLS | Tool schema. May need new optional fields for travel overrides (amount_of_motion, steps, guidance_scale, enhance_prompt) so the agent can override defaults. |

| reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/modelCapabilities.ts | MODEL_SPEC_REGISTRY, getModelSpec | Model-specific defaults (frames, steps, guidance). The agent needs these to compute correct per-model defaults. |

Documents all accepted fields — the contract the agent's output must satisfy. |

        ### Test Expectations
        - No explicit test expectations captured.

        ### Constraints
        - Must not break existing image generation context (imageContext) flow — travel context is additive
- The agent edge function runs in Deno (supabase/functions/) — cannot import frontend TS modules from src/ directly. Travel settings normalization logic must be duplicated or extracted to a shared edge-function-compatible module
- Shot-level settings are stored as JSONB in shots.settings['travel-between-images'] — must handle null/missing gracefully
- Amount of motion is 0-100 in UI settings but 0-1 in the resolver — must normalize when passing through
- LoRAs are stored as ShotLora[] (with id, name, path, strength) in settings but the resolver expects Array<{path, strength}> — must map
- Model name mapping: UI uses 'wan-2.2'/'ltx-2.3'/'ltx-2.3-fast', worker uses 'wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2'/'ltx2_22B'/'ltx2_22B_distilled' — normalizeTravelModelName() already handles this
- System prompt must stay concise — travel defaults section should be <100 tokens, only surface what the LLM needs to know (model, LoRA names, motion level)
- Tool schema additionalProperties is false — any new override fields must be explicitly added to the schema
- The create-task edge function must be redeployed after changes

        ### Suggested Approach
        Follow the imageContext pattern with a parallel travelContext:

1. **Types** (types.ts): Add `AgentTravelContext` interface with fields: `defaultModel: string`, `steps: number`, `frames: number`, `amountOfMotion: number`, `loras: Array<{name: string, path: string, strength: number}>`, `guidanceScale?: number`, `enhancePrompt: boolean`, `phaseConfig?: Record<string,unknown>`, `turboMode: boolean`, `negativePrompt?: string`, `textBeforePrompts?: string`, `textAfterPrompts?: string`.

2. **DB loading** (db.ts): Add `loadShotTravelSettings(supabaseAdmin, shotId)` that queries `shots.settings` for `['travel-between-images']` key, normalizes the JSONB (mirror the essential normalization from settings.ts), and returns `AgentTravelContext | null`. Also duplicate the model defaults constants (frames/steps per model) since we can't import from src/.

3. **Loop wiring** (loop.ts): After the existing imageContext block (~line 336), load travel settings using the already-resolved `clipShotId`. Build `travelContext` bag. Pass to both `buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt()` and `processToolCalls()`.

4. **System prompt** (prompts.ts): Add a travel defaults section when travelContext is present. Surface: preferred model, active LoRAs (by name), motion level, steps. Keep it brief — the LLM just needs to know what's configured so it can mention it and respect it.

5. **Tool schema** (tool-schemas.ts): Add optional fields to create_task for travel overrides: `amount_of_motion` (number 0-100), `steps` (number), `guidance_scale` (number), `enhance_prompt` (boolean). Don't expose all 30+ fields — just the ones a user would reasonably ask to change.

6. **create-task.ts**: Accept `travelContext?: AgentTravelContext`. When task_type is `image-to-video`, merge travelContext defaults into the params passed to `createGenerationTask()`. Agent-provided overrides take precedence over travelContext, which takes precedence over hardcoded defaults.

7. **generation.ts**: Expand `buildTravelBetweenImagesInput()` to accept and pass through all the new fields from params: loras, amount_of_motion (divide by 100), steps, guidance_scale, enhance_prompt, negative_prompts, phase_config, turbo_mode, text_before/after_prompts. The resolver already accepts all of these.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Make the timeline agent's travel-between-images (video travel) task creation match the VideoTravelForm behavior — load and use all default settings (model, LoRAs, motion params, etc.), surface them in the system prompt so the LLM knows what defaults are active, and allow the agent to override them based on user requests. Similar pattern to the image generation alignment done in make-the-timeline-agent-s-20260406-0602.

User notes and answers:
- ## Research Findings: Video Travel Settings & Agent Gap Analysis

### Where video travel settings are stored
- **Table:** shots.settings JSONB column, keyed by tool_id 'travel-between-images'
- **Scope:** Per-shot (shot > project > user > defaults cascade)
- **NOT in projects.settings** like image gen — this is a different storage path
- Settings loaded via useAutoSaveSettings → useToolSettings → shots.settings['travel-between-images']

### Key defaults the agent needs to know about
From settings.ts defaults (lines 158-194):
- selectedModel: 'wan-2.2' (options: wan-2.2, ltx-2.3, ltx-2.3-fast)
- batchVideoFrames: 61 (4N+1 format)
- batchVideoSteps: 6
- guidanceScale: undefined
- generationTypeMode: 'i2v' (i2v vs vace)
- amountOfMotion: 50 (0-100, converted to 0-1)
- motionMode: 'basic' vs 'advanced'
- turboMode: false
- smoothContinuations: false
- enhancePrompt: false
- loras: [] (Array<ShotLora> with id, name, path, strength, previewImageUrl, trigger_word)
- phaseConfig: undefined (advanced mode only)
- ltxHdResolution: true
- modelSettingsByModel: per-model frame/step overrides

### What the form sends to createGenerationTask (requestBody.ts lines 39-152)
- model_name (normalized)
- base_prompts, negative_prompts, text_before/after_prompts
- segment_frames, frame_overlap
- phase_config (if advanced mode)
- amount_of_motion
- loras: Array<{ path: string; strength: number }>
- pair_loras (per-pair overrides)
- turbo_mode, guidance_scale
- continuation_config (if smooth continuations)
- structure_guidance, travel_guidance
- generation_mode ('timeline', 'batch', 'by-pair')

### Current agent gaps for video travel
1. NO LoRA support at all — no schema field, no parsing, no loading, no passing
2. Model defaults not loaded from shot settings — always falls back to wan-2.2
3. No motion params (amountOfMotion, turboMode) exposed to agent
4. No frame/step count awareness
5. No guidance_scale support
6. No generationTypeMode (i2v vs vace) awareness
7. No smooth continuation support
8. image-to-video model default NOT pulled from imageContext (create-task.ts lines 133-135 only defaults for text-to-image)

### LoRA details for video travel
- ShotLora type: { id, name, path, strength, previewImageUrl?, trigger_word? }
- Stored in shots.settings['travel-between-images'].loras
- Multi-stage LoRAs have lowNoisePath for phase routing (high-noise early, low-noise final)
- Downstream create-task edge function DOES support loras param (travelBetweenImages.ts)
- Supports pair_loras for per-segment overrides

### How to load shot settings from the agent
The agent knows the shot context from selected clips (via resolveClipGenerationIds → findShotForGenerations).
To load shot settings: supabaseAdmin.from('shots').select('settings').eq('id', shotId).maybeSingle()
Then extract: data?.settings?.['travel-between-images']

### What the downstream create-task edge function accepts for travel
From supabase/functions/create-task/travelBetweenImages.ts:
- loras?: Array<{ path: string; strength: number }>
- pair_loras?: (Array<...> | null)[]
- model_name (normalized via normalizeTravelModelName)
- amount_of_motion
- turbo_mode
- guidance_scale
- phase_config
- continuation_config

### Standardization opportunity (USER REQUEST)
The image gen alignment and this video travel alignment share the same pattern:
1. Load tool-specific settings from DB
2. Surface defaults in system prompt
3. Thread settings through to task execution
4. Allow LLM to override based on user requests

Currently image gen uses:
- AgentProjectImageSettings type
- loadProjectImageSettings() from projects.settings
- imageContext bag with defaultModelName + activeReference
- Threaded through loop → processToolCalls → executeToolCall → executeCreateTask

For standardization, consider:
- Rename imageContext → agentToolContext or generationContext
- Add videoTravelSettings alongside image settings
- loadShotVideoTravelSettings() parallel to loadProjectImageSettings()
- Shared pattern: { defaults loaded from DB, surfaced in prompt, overridable by LLM }
- Single context bag that carries both image and video travel defaults
- buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt accepts both contexts
- executeCreateTask uses appropriate context based on task_type family

### Files to modify (agent-side)
All in supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/:
- types.ts — Add AgentVideoTravelSettings, rename/expand context types
- db.ts — Add loadShotVideoTravelSettings()
- tool-schemas.ts — Add loras, motion params, frame/step fields for i2v tasks
- prompts.ts — Surface video travel defaults in system prompt
- tools/create-task.ts — Pass video travel params through for i2v tasks
- loop.ts — Load video travel settings, build expanded context
- generation.ts — Accept and pass LoRA + motion params in buildTravelBetweenImagesInput

### Scope boundaries
- NOT doing: per-pair LoRA overrides (too complex for agent v1), structure guidance/ControlNet, steerable motion advanced settings, pair-level prompt overrides
- Phase config: Only expose through generationTypeMode (i2v/vace) toggle, not raw phase config editing
- LoRAs: expose what's configured in shot settings, allow LLM to note them, don't add/remove LoRAs
- USER REQUEST: Standardize the pattern between image gen settings and video travel settings. Don't just duplicate the imageContext pattern — refactor it so both share a common structure. Rename imageContext to something broader (e.g., generationContext) that carries both image and video travel defaults. The goal is one clean pattern that works for any tool's settings the agent needs to be aware of.

2. That plan only handles IMAGE LoRAs. We need to handle VIDEO TRAVEL LoRAs separately (stored in shots.settings, not projects.settings)

The form-side LoRA fix (steps 1-6 of that plan) stays independent, but the agent chain (steps 7-9) should be folded into this plan. This avoids two plans fighting over the same types/files.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Agent Video Travel Settings Alignment & Context Unification

## Overview

The timeline agent's `image-to-video` task creation hardcodes minimal parameters (segment_frames=[49], frame_overlap=[2]) and ignores shot-level VideoTravelSettings stored in `shots.settings['travel-between-images']`. The downstream resolver (`travelBetweenImages.ts`) already accepts all fields — the gap is purely in the agent's input construction.

**Goal:** Load shot-level travel settings from DB, surface them in the system prompt, thread them through task creation, and allow LLM overrides — while unifying the context pattern with image generation into a single `GenerationContext`.

**Key constraints:**
- The agent runs in Deno (edge functions) — cannot import from `src/`. Model defaults and normalization must be duplicated.
- Shot-only settings read is acceptable for v1 (the full defaults→user→project→shot cascade lives in frontend runtime code that cannot be imported into Deno; shot-level covers the primary use case).
- The existing image LoRA passthrough (`selectedLorasByCategory` on `CreateTaskImageContext`) is already shipped and must be preserved in the unified `GenerationContext`.

**Files touched (all under `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/`):**
- `types.ts` — new types, unified GenerationContext
- `db.ts` — new DB loader for shot travel settings
- `loop.ts` — lift shot resolution out of imageSettings gate, build unified context
- `prompts.ts` — surface travel defaults in system prompt
- `tool-schemas.ts` — new optional fields for travel overrides
- `tools/create-task.ts` — replace CreateTaskImageContext with GenerationContext, merge travel params
- `tools/generation.ts` — no explicit changes needed (legacyInput spread handles pass-through)
- `loop.test.ts` — update test to use new GenerationContext type

## Phase 1: Types & DB Loading

### Step 1: Add travel context types and unified GenerationContext (`types.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `AgentVideoTravelSettings` interface at `types.ts` after `AgentProjectImageSettings` (~line 108):
   ```ts
   export interface AgentVideoTravelSettings {
     selectedModel: string;          // 'wan-2.2' | 'ltx-2.3' | 'ltx-2.3-fast'
     frames: number;                 // batchVideoFrames (4N+1)
     steps: number;                  // batchVideoSteps
     amountOfMotion: number;         // 0-100 UI scale
     guidanceScale?: number;
     turboMode: boolean;
     enhancePrompt: boolean;
     negativePrompt?: string;
     textBeforePrompts?: string;
     textAfterPrompts?: string;
     generationTypeMode: 'i2v' | 'vace';
     generationMode: 'batch' | 'by-pair' | 'timeline';
     loras: Array<{ name: string; path: string; strength: number }>;
     phaseConfig?: Record<string, unknown>;
     smoothContinuations: boolean;
   }
   ```
2. **Add** unified `GenerationContext` type that preserves all existing `CreateTaskImageContext` fields:
   ```ts
   export interface GenerationContext {
     image: {
       defaultModelName?: AgentTextToImageModel;
       activeReference?: ResolvedReference | null;
       selectedLorasByCategory?: Partial<Record<"qwen" | "z-image", Array<{ path: string; strength: number }>>>;
     } | null;
     travel: AgentVideoTravelSettings | null;
   }
   ```
   Note: `image.selectedLorasByCategory` is explicitly included — this field is already shipped and functional in the current `CreateTaskImageContext`.

### Step 2: Add travel settings DB loader (`db.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `TRAVEL_MODEL_DEFAULTS` constant (duplicated from `src/tools/travel-between-images/modelCapabilities.ts` since we can't import):
   ```ts
   const TRAVEL_MODEL_DEFAULTS: Record<string, { frames: number; steps: number; guidanceScale?: number; workerModelName: string }> = {
     'wan-2.2': { frames: 61, steps: 6, workerModelName: 'wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2' },
     'ltx-2.3': { frames: 97, steps: 30, guidanceScale: 3, workerModelName: 'ltx2_22B' },
     'ltx-2.3-fast': { frames: 97, steps: 8, guidanceScale: 3, workerModelName: 'ltx2_22B_distilled' },
   };
   const VALID_TRAVEL_MODELS = Object.keys(TRAVEL_MODEL_DEFAULTS);
   ```
2. **Add** `loadShotVideoTravelSettings(supabaseAdmin, shotId)` that:
   - Queries `supabaseAdmin.from('shots').select('settings').eq('id', shotId).maybeSingle()`
   - Extracts `data?.settings?.['travel-between-images']`
   - Normalizes into `AgentVideoTravelSettings` using the same helpers already in db.ts (`asTrimmedString`, `asOptionalNumber`, `asOptionalBoolean`, `isRecord`)
   - Resolves effective frames/steps: check `modelSettingsByModel[selectedModel]` first, then fall back to `TRAVEL_MODEL_DEFAULTS[selectedModel]`
   - Normalizes LoRAs: filter to entries with valid `id`, `name`, `path`, `strength` fields, map to `{name, path, strength}`
   - Returns `null` if no settings exist or shotId is null/undefined
3. **Handle** gracefully: if query errors, log warning and return `null` (non-fatal — agent falls back to hardcoded defaults).

### Step 3: Add tool schema fields for travel overrides (`tool-schemas.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** optional fields to `create_task` properties (at `tool-schemas.ts:91`, before `additionalProperties: false`):
   - `amount_of_motion`: `{ type: "number", description: "Motion intensity 0-100 for image-to-video. Higher = more motion." }`
   - `steps`: `{ type: "number", description: "Inference steps for image-to-video. Model-dependent (wan-2.2: 6, ltx-2.3: 30, ltx-2.3-fast: 8)." }`
   - `guidance_scale`: `{ type: "number", description: "Guidance scale for image-to-video (LTX models only)." }`
   - `enhance_prompt`: `{ type: "boolean", description: "Auto-enhance prompts for image-to-video." }`
   - `turbo_mode`: `{ type: "boolean", description: "Turbo mode for wan-2.2 image-to-video (faster, lower quality)." }`

LoRAs are NOT exposed as a tool arg — they come from shot settings only. The agent can mention configured LoRAs in conversation but cannot add/remove them.

## Phase 2: Wiring & Integration

### Step 4: Lift shot resolution out of imageSettings gate and build unified context (`loop.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium

This step addresses the critical bug where `clipShotId` is only computed when `imageSettings` is truthy (FLAG-002).

1. **Import** `GenerationContext` and `AgentVideoTravelSettings` from `types.ts`. Import `loadShotVideoTravelSettings` from `db.ts`. Remove `CreateTaskImageContext` import from `tools/create-task.ts`.
2. **Lift** `resolveClipGenerationIds` and `findShotForGenerations` out of the `imageSettings` gate. The current code at `loop.ts:322-327`:
   ```ts
   // BEFORE (gated):
   const clipGenerationIds = imageSettings
     ? resolveClipGenerationIds(effectiveSelectedClips ?? [], timelineState.registry, timelineState.config)
     : [];
   const clipShotId = imageSettings && clipGenerationIds.length > 0
     ? await findShotForGenerations(supabaseAdmin, clipGenerationIds)
     : null;
   ```
   Becomes:
   ```ts
   // AFTER (always resolved):
   const clipGenerationIds = resolveClipGenerationIds(effectiveSelectedClips ?? [], timelineState.registry, timelineState.config);
   const clipShotId = clipGenerationIds.length > 0
     ? await findShotForGenerations(supabaseAdmin, clipGenerationIds)
     : null;
   ```
3. **Load** travel settings after shot resolution:
   ```ts
   const travelSettings = clipShotId
     ? await loadShotVideoTravelSettings(supabaseAdmin, clipShotId)
     : null;
   ```
4. **Build** unified `GenerationContext`:
   ```ts
   const generationContext: GenerationContext = {
     image: imageSettings
       ? {
         defaultModelName: imageSettings.selectedTextModel ?? "qwen-image",
         activeReference,
         selectedLorasByCategory: imageSettings.selectedLorasByCategory,
       }
       : null,
     travel: travelSettings,
   };
   ```
5. **Update** `buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt()` call to pass `travelSettings`.
6. **Update** `processToolCalls()` and `executeToolCall()` signatures: replace `imageContext?: CreateTaskImageContext` with `generationContext?: GenerationContext`. Thread `generationContext` through to `executeCreateTask()`.

### Step 5: Surface travel defaults in system prompt (`prompts.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `travelSettings?: AgentVideoTravelSettings | null` to `buildTimelineAgentSystemPrompt()` options parameter (at `prompts.ts:36`).
2. **Build** a compact travel defaults line when travelSettings is non-null (insert after `activeReferenceLine` block, before the main prompt template around `prompts.ts:69`):
   ```
   Active video travel settings: model={selectedModel}, frames={frames}, steps={steps}, motion={amountOfMotion}%{loraLine}{guidanceLine}
   Use these defaults for image-to-video tasks. The user can ask to change motion, model, or steps.
   ```
   - Only include LoRA names if `loras.length > 0`: `, loras=[Name1, Name2]`
   - Only include guidance if set: `, guidance={guidanceScale}`
3. **Keep under ~100 tokens** — only surface what the LLM needs for conversation context.

### Step 6: Thread travel context through create-task (`tools/create-task.ts`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** `CreateTaskImageContext` interface with import of `GenerationContext` from `types.ts`.
2. **Update** `executeCreateTask` signature: `imageContext?: CreateTaskImageContext` → `generationContext?: GenerationContext`.
3. **Extract** sub-contexts at top of function:
   ```ts
   const imageContext = generationContext?.image ?? null;
   const travelContext = generationContext?.travel ?? null;
   ```
4. **Update** all existing `imageContext?.` references to use the extracted local (no behavior change — same fields, just nested one level deeper).
5. **For** `image-to-video` task type, build travel params with correct normalization. **Critical: both LLM overrides AND context values must be on 0-1 scale when passed downstream** (addresses FLAG-003):
   ```ts
   const rawMotion = typeof args.amount_of_motion === 'number' ? args.amount_of_motion : undefined;
   const travelParams = taskType === 'image-to-video' ? {
     // amount_of_motion: ALWAYS divide by 100 — schema tells LLM 0-100, resolver expects 0-1
     amount_of_motion: rawMotion !== undefined ? rawMotion / 100 : (travelContext ? travelContext.amountOfMotion / 100 : undefined),
     steps: asPositiveNumber(args.steps) ?? travelContext?.steps,
     guidance_scale: asFiniteNumber(args.guidance_scale) ?? travelContext?.guidanceScale,
     enhance_prompt: typeof args.enhance_prompt === 'boolean' ? args.enhance_prompt : travelContext?.enhancePrompt,
     turbo_mode: typeof args.turbo_mode === 'boolean' ? args.turbo_mode : travelContext?.turboMode,
     loras: travelContext?.loras?.map(l => ({ path: l.path, strength: l.strength })),
     negative_prompts: travelContext?.negativePrompt ? [travelContext.negativePrompt] : undefined,
     text_before_prompts: travelContext?.textBeforePrompts || undefined,
     text_after_prompts: travelContext?.textAfterPrompts || undefined,
     segment_frames: travelContext ? [travelContext.frames] : undefined,
     // Use resolver's actual field names (addresses FLAG-004):
     model_type: travelContext?.generationTypeMode,  // 'i2v' | 'vace' — maps directly to resolver's model_type
     generation_mode: travelContext?.generationMode ?? 'timeline',  // Default to 'timeline' not 'batch' (addresses FLAG-006)
     phase_config: travelContext?.phaseConfig,
   } : undefined;
   ```
   **Note on smooth continuations:** The form builds `continuation_config` from policy resolution (`resolveContinuationPolicy`), which requires model spec lookups that are too complex to replicate in the agent. For v1, we omit `continuation_config` — the resolver handles missing continuation config gracefully (no continuation). This is a known limitation for the agent path.
6. **Pass** travelParams via `params` field to `createGenerationTask()`:
   ```ts
   params: taskType === 'image-to-video' && travelParams
     ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(travelParams).filter(([, v]) => v !== undefined))
     : (mergedParams && Object.keys(mergedParams).length > 0 ? mergedParams : undefined),
   ```
7. **Default model** for image-to-video: use `travelContext?.selectedModel` when agent doesn't specify:
   ```ts
   const defaultModelName = taskType === 'image-to-video'
     ? travelContext?.selectedModel
     : (taskType === "text-to-image" || Boolean(TASK_TYPE_TO_REFERENCE_MODE[taskType]))
       ? imageContext?.defaultModelName
       : undefined;
   ```

### Step 7: No changes needed to generation.ts
**Scope:** None — removed from plan.

`buildTravelBetweenImagesInput()` at `generation.ts:123-146` already spreads `legacyInput` (which is `args.params`). All fields merged into `params` in Step 6 will flow through automatically via the spread at line 138-139:
```ts
return {
  ...legacyInput,  // ← This spread carries all travel params through
  image_urls: imageUrls,
  base_prompts: ...,
  segment_frames: ...,
  ...
};
```

The `segment_frames` fallback `[49]` at line 142 only applies when `legacyInput.segment_frames` is not an array — when travelContext provides `[61]` via params, it arrives as an array in legacyInput and takes precedence. Same for `frame_overlap` at line 143 (defaults to `[2]` when not provided).

## Phase 3: Tests & Validation

### Step 8: Update loop.test.ts (`loop.test.ts`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Update** `executeToolCall` calls in the test (at `loop.test.ts:60-94`) to pass the new 6th argument as `undefined` (was the 6th arg `imageContext`, now `generationContext`). The test currently passes `undefined` implicitly by not providing the 6th arg — if the function signature changes to require it or the type changes, update accordingly.
2. **Update** the `executeCreateTask` mock assertion at `loop.test.ts:81-87`: the 5th argument was `undefined` (imageContext), now should be `undefined` (generationContext). Verify mock expectations match new arity.
3. **Key invariant:** The test for `create_task` dispatch at line 80-87 currently expects `executeCreateTask` called with `(args, timelineState, selectedClips, supabaseAdmin, undefined)`. After the refactor, the same call should pass `undefined` as the 5th arg (generationContext) when no context is provided in the test.

### Step 9: Remove old CreateTaskImageContext export
**Scope:** Small
1. **Remove** `CreateTaskImageContext` interface from `tools/create-task.ts:58-62`.
2. **Remove** the `CreateTaskImageContext` import from `loop.ts:35`.
3. **Verify** no other files import `CreateTaskImageContext` (it was only used in loop.ts and create-task.ts).

### Step 10: Compile check and manual smoke test
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** `cd reigh-app && npx tsc --noEmit` to verify TypeScript compiles.
2. **Manual test scenarios:**
   - Agent creates image-to-video task with no shot settings → falls back to hardcoded defaults (wan-2.2, 49 frames, 2 overlap). Behavior unchanged.
   - Agent creates image-to-video task with shot settings (ltx-2.3, LoRAs, motion=75) → all fields threaded through with correct normalization (motion=0.75, model_type='i2v', generation_mode='timeline').
   - Agent overrides amount_of_motion=30 via tool arg → sends 0.3 to resolver (not 30).
   - System prompt shows travel defaults when settings exist.
   - Image generation tasks still work identically (image LoRAs, reference, model selection all unchanged).
3. **Deploy:** `npx supabase functions deploy ai-timeline-agent --project-ref wczysqzxlwdndgxitrvc`

## Execution Order
1. Types first (Step 1) — everything depends on these.
2. DB loader (Step 2) — independent, needed by loop.
3. Tool schema (Step 3) — independent, needed for agent overrides.
4. Loop wiring (Step 4) — depends on types + DB loader. Critical: must lift shot resolution out of imageSettings gate.
5. System prompt (Step 5) — depends on types.
6. Create-task threading (Step 6) — depends on unified context shape.
7. Tests + cleanup (Steps 8-9) — after all functional changes.
8. Compile + smoke test (Step 10) — final validation.

## Validation Order
1. TypeScript compilation after Steps 1-9.
2. Run existing tests after Step 8.
3. Manual smoke tests in Step 10.

## Known Limitations (v1)
- **Settings cascade:** Shot-only read — does not replicate the frontend's defaults→user→project→shot cascade. This is acceptable because the cascade runtime lives in frontend-only code. Shot-level covers the primary use case where users have configured travel settings.
- **Smooth continuations:** Not threaded through. The form builds `continuation_config` from policy resolution which requires complex model spec lookups. The resolver handles missing continuation config gracefully.
- **Per-pair overrides:** Not supported (too complex for agent v1). The agent creates uniform segments.

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    }
  ],

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 3,
    "plan_count": 1,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

        Project directory:
